- **freight-quote-analysis**: 把公司成本、最低可报、合同价、历史成交价和准备报给客户的价格放在一起比较，算出大概能赚多少并建议怎么报。支持专线固定价、城配多点加价、冷链温控加价。当用户说“这票怎么报”“这个价格能不能接”或“帮我写报价给客户”时使用。 ([WeaveQ/OnMyAgent](https://github.com/WeaveQ/OnMyAgent/tree/HEAD/apps/desktop/resources/marketplace/experts/plugins/order-dispatch-specialist/skills/freight-quote-analysis) · ★6)

- **freminal-config-options**: Use ONLY when working in the freminal repository AND adding, renaming, or removing a configuration option (a field on `Config` or any of its section structs in `freminal-common/src/config.rs`, e.g. a new `[notifications]`/`[command_blocks]` key or a whole new section). - **freminal-cursor-affordances**: Use ONLY when working in the freminal repository AND adding or debugging anything that changes the mouse cursor, or adding a new clickable/draggable element to the GUI — a chrome widget (button, menu entry, tab, checkbox, combo box, link), a hover affordance on the terminal surface (URL, command-block gutter, fold placeholder), or a drag handle (pane divider). - **freminal-damage-model**: Use ONLY when working in the freminal repository AND adding or changing anything that writes or derives `PaneFrameDamage`, `FrameDamage`, `ChromeDamage`, any `*_changed` signal feeding them, `PresentRegion`, `DamageHistory`, or clear/clip/present-region/full-repaint logic.
